#### Deployment Log v3.100
**Files deployed:** decision-telemetry-emission (cross-cutting class) — one release PR #4187 (`release/decision-telemetry-emission → main`, merge-commit `35f71ae7`), 6 delivery slices (#3712, #3704, #4051, #3723, #4025, #4026) on one branch, 30 commits, 32 files (+2603/−72). The release ships the **hub write-side** of decision telemetry end-to-end: the event-emission tooling (`release/tools/append-pipeline-event.sh`, `release/tools/query-pipeline-event.sh`, `release/tools/check-emission-contract-subset.sh`), the schema extension that gives decision events a stable join key (`release/references/standards/pipeline-event-log-schema.md`, founded by `release/ADRs/ADR-086-event-log-schema-decision-subtype-extension.md` and `release/ADRs/ADR-100-event-log-payload-pipe-grammar.md` with its `core/ADRs/README.md` index row), the autonomous-seam definitions (`core/disciplines/autonomous-execution-model.md`, `core/disciplines/decision-discipline.md`), the hub playbook + briefing surfaces that perform the emission (`release/skills/release-hub/SKILL.md`, `references/orchestration-playbook.md`, `references/decision-briefing.md`, and `release/references/how-to/hub-spoke-bridge.md`), the gate-efficacy and hub-action-tracking standards (`core/standards/gate-efficacy-standard.md`, `core/standards/hub-action-tracking.md`), the Stage-12/13 pipeline specs that anchor the emit points (`release/references/pipeline/stage-12-execute.md`, `stage-13-close.md`, `release/references/standards/deployment-cycle-time.md`), the enforcement scaffolding (`.github/decision-emission.enforce`, `core/deploy/allowlists/decision-emission-asserted-set.txt`, `core/deploy/deploy.sh` Check 19/61, `core/deploy/lib-instance-path.sh`, `.github/workflows/release-tooling-smoke.yml`), the test surfaces (`core/deploy/tests/test_check19_event_log_integrity.sh`, `release/tools/tests/test_action_item_gate_predicate.sh`), plus `core/standards/depersonalization-spec.md` and the `finops-usage-extractor` rollup script with both rebuilt `.skill` packages. **Four vacuous controls** the pipeline caught — Check 19a (stale path; 19b/19c had never run in repo history), the Procedure-7a gate, the CIAC-3 lint (zero call sites), and CIAC-1's unfunded predicate — each now armed with a falsification test.
**Mechanism:** git merge-commit (PR #4187 → main at `35f71ae70678a1e2a61d80a02c7a7a5f8191d009`, **plain `gh pr merge --merge`, no `--admin` required** — branch protection did not block this release, `required_approving_review_count: 0`; a true two-parent merge commit — parents `e8e94810` (main) and `e7846ab1` (release head) — so the `git revert -m 1` rollback convention applies); signed-annotated tag `v3.100` at the merge SHA (annotated tag object `ec99098c`, object type `tag`, SSH-signed ED25519, `git tag -v` → *Good signature*; unique claim bound at the Stage-12 atomic ref-CAS via `claim-version.sh --sha 35f71ae7 --bump minor`, **first-attempt win with zero CAS collisions and zero signing bypasses**). **Pre-merge gate evidence:** Phase A.4 skipped per A.4.1 (no merge strategy declared in the plan; allowed-strategies map `{merge:true, rebase:true, squash:true}` recorded for the audit trail), Phase A.5.1 divergence **13** commits behind main with conflict surface **0**, resolved by the merge commit itself (`MERGEABLE/CLEAN` is the server-side computation of the same no-conflict assertion), **Phase A.5.6b FREE** — the adapter dry-run recomputed next-free = `v3.100`, equal to the carried version, so no re-version was needed; Phase A.6.2 PASS (`MERGEABLE/CLEAN`, polling loop not entered), Phase A.7.2 ready (`isDraft=false`), Phase B0.2 **zero** dependent PRs (skip base-shift; Option A default). **Four-surface version-freeness enumeration at B0** (this release had been overtaken three times — v3.97, v3.98, v3.99 each claimed by a sibling mid-pipeline): origin tags max `v3.99`, published Releases max `v3.99`, `RELEASE_LOG` rows read from `origin/main` max `v3.99` with **zero** `DEPLOYED`-not-`VERIFIED` in-flight rows, and all three unmerged `release/*` sibling branches inspected — `release/agent-finops-intelligence` is **PR-less and slug-primary** (4 unresolved `{{RELEASE_VERSION}}` tokens, no tag, no `DEPLOYED` row: holds no claim — this is the branch class invisible to a tag/Release/open-PR scan that caused an earlier collision this release), and `release/v3.97-decision-audit-and-learning` (PR #4167, draft) carries a **stale** `v3.97` label against an already-`VERIFIED` slot and will need re-versioning at its own Stage 12. **ADR-092 claim-time stamp — mechanism deviation, recorded:** the claim ran **without** `--stamp-slug`, so the post-CAS stamping pass did not fire; the stamp was instead performed inside this Stage-12 chore PR (both `{{RELEASE_VERSION}}` tokens resolved to `v3.100`, plan git-mv'd from the slug-primary path to its post-claim home `release/releases/plans/v3/v3.100_RELEASE_PLAN.md`; zero tokens survive). Rationale: `claim-version.sh`'s stamp seam commits with `git push origin HEAD`, which from a session worktree resolves to the worktree's own branch rather than `main`, and a direct-to-main stamp push is forbidden by `core/rules/git-workflow.md` § "What NOT To Do"; the chore-PR path lands byte-identical content through the governed gate. **AI-013 post-claim rebuild obligation fired** (unlike v3.99): the branch carried `release-hub` frontmatter `version: v3.99` deliberately left at main's value, so the claimed `v3.100` required a re-version plus a package rebuild **in the same commit** — both landed here, verified by a direct run of `deploy.sh --check-package-freshness` reading its **verdict text**, not its exit code: `package-freshness: 54 rostered skill package(s) content-fresh — OK`.
**Timestamp:** 2026-07-28 (release merge 2026-07-29T03:52:56Z UTC; signed-annotated tag `v3.100` at the merge SHA, tagger 2026-07-29T03:53:43Z UTC — the merge instant falls after 00:00 UTC, so the UTC calendar date is 2026-07-29 while the operator-local deploy date is Tuesday 2026-07-28)
**Cycle-Time:** N/A — (T_GO=n/a; T_DEPLOY=n/a; mechanism: compute-cycle-time.sh) — the tool reports no `gate-outcome/plan-review-go` anchor and no `deployment-status/deploy-skill` or `deploy-harness` event logged for v3.100, which is the standard's defined `N/A` condition rather than a missing measurement. This is the structurally-expected result for this release specifically: the reflexive exemption means the write side this release ships does not emit for its own gates, so no anchor event can exist yet. Recorded as `N/A` with mechanism rather than estimated — #3723 AC-5 is documented, not delivered.
**Velocity:** planned 20 pts / delivered 20 pts (1.00); files-changed 32; allocation 0/20/0 pts (feature/debt/protocol-slack); class cross-cutting (mechanism: `compute-release-velocity.sh v3.100 --milestone 295 --merge-sha 35f71ae7`; computed at Stage 13 with all six delivery slices already closed by the release PR, so delivered is authoritative. **Allocation reads 0/20/0 despite a 3-bug / 3-story membership split** — the three `type:bug` slices (#4051 `size:S`, #3712 `size:S`, #3704 `size:M`) map to debt directly, and the three `type:story` slices (#4026, #4025, #3723, all `size:M`) carry `improvement` rather than `enhancement` / `type:feature`, which the label→work-class map does not recognise as a feature signal, so they fall to the conservative debt default. The **bucket** is defensible on the merits — all six slices repair the pipeline's own instrumentation and self-checking surfaces rather than adding user-facing capability — but the **route** is a mapping gap, not a work-class claim: an `improvement`-labelled story cannot currently register as feature allocation. Recorded here rather than silently accepted; same class of finding as v3.98's `type:story` artifact.)
**Result:** SUCCESS — release PR #4187 merged to main via a true two-parent merge commit; `v3.100` signed-annotated tag valid at the merge SHA (object type `tag`, not `commit`; `git tag -v` → *Good signature*); CI **47/47 checks non-failing** on the live head at the merge instant, `MERGEABLE/CLEAN`, `isDraft=false`. Zero signing bypasses, zero CAS collisions, zero force pushes.
**Outcome:** **ALIGNED, UNOBSERVED** — finalized at this Stage 13 VERIFIED transition, unchanged from the Stage-12 read. Assessed against the Release Outcome Statement — a conformance limit, not a shortfall, and deliberately **not** recorded as delivered. The Statement promised that every release would emit a minimum decision-event set whose rows join 1:1 to the shipped release. **This release ships the complete mechanism; it does not yet ship the outcome.** The reflexive exemption leaves the write side **inert until the next release** — live event population **0**, Check 61 **SKIP**, falsification **fixture-only** — so the Success Indicator is unobservable until the next release runs, and the three Operator Override Records rendered at the Stage 9 GO gate (#3723 AC-3, #4025 AC-2, #4025 AC-3) all share that single root cause: behaviour that cannot be observed until the write side goes live. Residual risk accepted at GO and carried into deploy: Check 61 has no pre-merge surface (a release can close with zero events and green CI); CIAC-1's guard is CI-unfunded (#3702); CIAC-3's lint exits 0 on an empty asserted set while Check 61 treats that as a repo defect — two controls this release ships disagree; and DT-W3-01's unenforced MUST has an exemption bound that **expires when the write side goes live**. The next release is this release's first real test.

#### Deployment Log v3.99
**Files deployed:** release-hub-response-convention-enforcement (novel class) — one release PR #4171 (`release/release-hub-response-convention-enforcement → main`, merge-commit `3f8af4a7`), 2 delivery slices (#4020 `size:S`, #4021 `size:M`) on one branch (serial authoring #4020 → #4021), 17 commits, 5 files (+551/−10). Both slices are **conformance defects** against the `release-hub` skill's own runtime behaviour, found by an agent-response governance audit. **#4020** — decision-class emissions omitted the mandated reversibility tier + confidence label (41/75 = 54.7% conformance) because the `## Output Contract` scope clause bound all requirements to *"every **Mode R** emission"*, leaving Mode O emissions with no labeling obligation at all. The fix widens the opener to bind **both modes**, marks the genuinely Mode-R-scoped requirements inside the numbered list so widening does not wrongly bind a Mode O turn to the readiness-check schema, retargets requirement 5 from "the milestone verdict" to *every decision-class emission in either mode*, and adds an **emission-time predicate** paragraph — one question posed while composing (*does this turn ask the operator to decide, approve, authorize, or act?*), a closed enumeration of the hub's decision-class emissions keyed to its Procedure vocabulary, an explicit operational residual making that list a floor rather than a ceiling, a ceremony bound naming what carries no label, the literal `[TIER · confidence: LEVEL]` format at its point of use, and the disqualifier that prose reasoning about reversibility does not satisfy the requirement. **#4021** — the render-before-`AskUserQuestion` briefing gate and the `Stage · gate · progress` anchor (89/117 = 76.1% conformance). Gate 3 of `references/decision-briefing.md` gains an emission-time self-gate that cites item-1 content **by reference** (rather than enumerating its fields, which would drift against #4020's new field), names **declared deferral** as the legitimate consolidated-briefing form, and adds a falsifiable deferral-vs-skip discriminator; the file's declared Mode-O-only scope is reconciled at its H1 and intro so it stops carrying both-mode obligations under a single-mode title. A net-new anchor requirement makes the one-line `Stage · gate · progress` opener **non-waivable on short mid-stream turns** — outage notes, retries, tool-failure diagnostics, post-write read-backs, terse acknowledgments, intent-to-gather micro-turns — the exact turn shapes where the anchor is observably dropped, with a matching Domain-Specific Failure Mode entry authored to the 5-field template under category **OUT**. **Concurrent-release renumber (the notable event of this release):** v3.98 independently added its *own* Output-Contract requirement 7 (the readiness map) and made the same `Six` → `Seven` count bump. Resolved at the pre-B1 merge by evidence, not preference — v3.98's requirement 7 **keeps slot 7** because it self-references its own number in its body text and its `references/readiness-map-template.md` cites "Output-Contract requirement 7" twice, so renumbering it would have edited shipped prose and broken a live reference cascade; this release's anchor had **zero inbound citations by number** and renumbered to **8** at zero cost. The count word reconciles to **`Eight`** — a value neither release specified alone — with membership Mode-R-scoped `{2,3,4,6,7}` and both-modes `{1,5,8}`. A `fix(dt)` commit landed **pre-B1** restating the frozen plan's requirement-7 references (including its AC-2, which post-merge literally described v3.98's requirement) against requirement 8; the mechanical CIAC probes passed either way, so nothing else would have caught it. Phase B4: `deploy.sh --deploy release-hub` propagated the single changed skill to the runtime skills root — SKILL.md, the `references/` mirror (5 files), and the `.skill` package — scoped to exactly the one skill this release touched rather than a full-roster redeploy; Check 1 (skill sync) reports `OK: release-hub`, `OK: release-hub references/ (5 files)`, `OK: release-hub.skill` against the merged tree. **Result:** SUCCESS — release PR #4171 merged to main via a true two-parent merge commit; v3.99 signed-annotated tag valid at the merge SHA; CI **41/41 pass** on the live head `8e609d7c` at the merge instant, MERGEABLE/CLEAN, `isDraft=false`. Pre-merge gates all cleared: Phase A.4 skipped per A.4.1 (no merge strategy declared in the plan; allowed-strategies map `{merge:true, rebase:true, squash:true}` recorded for the audit trail), **Phase A.5.1 divergence 4** — the v3.98 close-out chores, commit-scoped to `RELEASE_LOG.md` only and zero-overlap with this release's 5-file change matrix — resolved by **Phase A.5.3 clean merge** (`8e609d7c`, no conflicts) leaving 0 behind, **Phase A.5.6b FREE** (adapter dry-run recomputed next-free = `v3.99`, equal to the carried version — no re-version needed; independently cross-checked against the tag maximum `v3.98`), Phase A.6.2 PASS (MERGEABLE/CLEAN, polling loop not entered), **Phase A.7 transition executed here** (the PR was still `isDraft=true` on entry — the Stage 9 Phase A8 draft→ready transition had not been applied — so it was transitioned before B1 exactly as the guard's remediation path specifies), Phase B0.2 **zero** dependent PRs (skip base-shift; Option A default, no stacked-base posture declared). **Cross-PR re-check (A6.5) re-run immediately before B1:** the open-PR population changed four times during this release; PR #4187 (`decision-telemetry-emission`) overlaps 4 of this release's 5 files but was `state=OPEN`, `mergedAt=null`, `mergeable=CONFLICTING` at the claim instant — the collision never materialised and no fresh resolution was attempted inside the Execute phase. Mainline carries exactly **1** `deploy.sh --check` FAIL — Check 47 `release-body-drift`, **pre-existing**, owned by **#3699**, at an unchanged finding count of **5** (only the denominator moved 20 → 21 as v3.98's row landed); severity-slot probe `^\[[0-9:]+\] +FAIL:` → 1 with a same-form `WARN:` control → **391**, proving the probe shape matches real severity slots rather than silently matching nothing. This release introduced zero new FAILs and did not attempt remediation of #3699.
**Outcome:** SUCCESS — **PARTIALLY-ATTAINED** against the Release Outcome Statement, by design rather than by shortfall. The Statement's AFTER is a *runtime-behaviour* claim (release-hub emits every decision-class turn carrying its mandated tier + confidence label, renders the full 4-part briefing before each gate with legitimate deferrals preserved, and anchors interrupt / outage / transitional turns), and its Success Indicator is a re-audit rate. Post-deploy `main` exhibits the full **mechanism** half of the AFTER state — verified structurally, each probe with a live negative control: `Eight requirements` = 1, `hold on every emission` = 1, `Requirements 1, 5, and 8` = 1, the emission-time predicate `decide, approve, authorize, or act` = 1, and `reversibility tier + confidence` = 1 in the briefing contract's item 1; the pre-fix forms are gone — `Six requirements` = 0, `hold on every Mode R emission` = 0, `Requirements 1, 5, and 7` = 0. Gate 3 carries both the **Declared deferral** form and the falsifiable deferral-vs-skip discriminator (2 hits, case-insensitive probe; the case-sensitive form returned 0 and was corrected rather than reported as absent). What `main` cannot exhibit is the **behavioural** half: the release is introducing-release-exempt from its own emission rules, so the population the Success Indicator measures — post-cutover runtime turns — did not exist at close. Both rate ACs are therefore recorded `declared-verification-deferred` per operator decision **D-ACGradeability** into a 30-day outcome window (opens 2026-07-29 Wednesday, closes 2026-08-28 Friday), method = Round-2 `agent-response-governance-audit`, gated on #4169 (amended) landing first. **Routed (C) accept-as-residual** per QC4-06 — the deferral was a planned Stage-4 decision, not an unmet objective, and the epic (#4019) does not close on this release alone. **Round-2 read-guard:** against the frozen instrument a perfectly-conforming agent ceilings at 70/75 = 93.3% (AC-2) and 100/117 = 85.5% (AC-3), both below their own thresholds; classify non-conformers by shape before comparing to the bar — rule-exempt shapes ⇒ instrument over-inclusion, do not reopen; ask-shaped turns missing a label or sitting in the silence cell ⇒ genuine fix failure.

**Surprise:** the `.version` bump was directed SKIPPED on the reading that a later, already-tagged release (v3.99) would be regressed by stamping v3.98. Both governing checks anchor elsewhere: Check 39 anchors the latest **published** GitHub Release (v3.99 is tag-only — `gh release view v3.99` returns *release not found*), and Check 48 anchors the most-recent **VERIFIED** `RELEASE_LOG` row (v3.98 once this close lands). Stamping v3.98 was therefore correct, and skipping it would have *manufactured* a Check 48 finding rather than avoided one.
**Would-change:** treat "a higher version is already tagged" as the prompt to read the two anchors, not as evidence of regression risk. Check 39's own rationale block explicitly rejects tag/semver-max anchoring and names the tagged-but-unpublished-ahead case as the exact situation it refuses to fail — the deciding evidence was already codified and only needed reading.
**Watch-for:** `automated-closeout.sh` emits **neither** the Phase B-velocity `**Velocity:**` field **nor** the Phase A7 learnings triple, though `stage-13-close.md` mandates both in the same Stage 13 chore PR. A pure-script close drops two codified outputs, and Check 48's asserted set does not include either — so the gap is invisible to the standing completeness gate and only surfaces when a closer compares against sibling rows by hand. Also recurring: ADR-099 still reads `status: Proposed` after its Stage 9 ratification gate.

Built in four serialized waves on a single branch (P0 fully-serial, operator-ratified). **Wave A — the `deploy.sh` check cluster.** **#1039** adds **Check 55** (work-hierarchy drift) over the net-new `core/deploy/tools/check-work-hierarchy.py`: an H1 doc-invariant leg (banned parent tier, SSOT-derived vocabulary, citation-guarded) and an H2 backlog leg (epic-under-epic via one paginated GraphQL query). The H1 predicate was **narrowed at build time** from "any token outside the licensed kind vocabulary" to the ADR-049 closed banned-tier set `{Initiative, Roadmap}` after the broad form measured 8 findings / 8 false positives (the KM altitude ladder, the SAFe row of the Layer-2 methodology map, ADR-018's projection, and ADR-049's own lowercase label-mapping title) — flagging the SSOT map for containing methodology tier names is incoherent. **#2219** adds **Check 56** (milestone↔epic membership) over `core/deploy/tools/check-milestone-epic-membership.py`: M1 membership (enforce-capable, override-able) + M2 description↔membership reconciliation (warn-only regardless of resolved mode, per the Stage-5 asymmetric-severity design); placement resolved by **D-A** to `deploy.sh --check` rather than `repo-integrity.yml` (milestone↔epic membership is repo-state, not a PR-diff property). **#2682** widens **Check 16** in place — the single fetch drops `--label improvement` to cover all open intake, invariant I2 gains a `type:epic` + `sub-task` exemption, and the check is **decoupled** onto its own `.mode` file so a shared enforce-flip elsewhere cannot enforce the untested broadened net; `core/specs/label-taxonomy.md` Rule 2 gains a per-type status-label applicability table and Rule 6 is restated as a type exemption, and `release/references/specs/ticket-information-architecture.md` was reconciled in the same commit (widening Check 16 rendered its Invariant-Enforcement table factually false — the identical self-contradicting-doc defect Stage 5 flagged for `label-taxonomy.md`, in a second file the spec's grep missed). **#2106** adds **Check 57** (extraction-contract validity) over `core/deploy/tools/check-extraction-contract.py`, asserting `log "Check N:"` emitters ↔ `# Check N` definition blocks entirely inside `deploy.sh` with **no doc-side marker** — the original enumeration-parity premise died with the commit that resolved its prerequisite by *removing* the enumeration in favour of a derive-from-source pointer, so re-adding a marker would have re-created the duplicate surface that removal eliminated (**D-C**). **Wave B — #2685** extends dead-reference coverage to `core/ADRs/`, `core/deploy/`, repo-root `*.md` and `.github/` (excluding `release/releases/`, already covered recursively), and — beyond either Stage-5 option — factors the previously byte-identical inline `--target-paths` string out of `deploy.sh` Check 14 and `link-check.yml` into **one shared file** read by both via a new `--target-paths-file` flag, making divergence structurally impossible rather than convention-enforced; `release/tools/check-release-links.py` `is_skippable` is broadened to treat all three live release-artifact filename shapes consistently and gains its first `--self-test`, wired as a hard precision probe in CI. **Wave C — #1490 + #3009.** #1490 ships `release/tools/check-adr-durability.py` (+565, 30-case self-test) and an `adr-durability` job on `repo-integrity.yml`: R1 status-enum (leading-token rule), R2 stale-anchor (no hardcoded SHA, no live population count in durable prose), R3 operator-handle. R2 precision was **measured, not asserted** — a naive predicate scored 41 corpus findings with a ~85% false-positive tail; three self-tested structural guards (plural-only nouns plus the exemplar shape, no identifier prefix, closed reference-word prefix set) cut it to 6 genuine. #3009 ships **G-CL9** (the authority — a Gate 13 criterion + self-repair row in `core/schemas/gate-criteria-spec.md`, schema minor-bumped additively, plus the Stage 13 Close narrative in `release/governance/release-process.md` carrying the surgical status-field-and-provenance-only constraint) and **Check 58** (the backstop, structurally never enforce-capable — a dedicated advisory-only emitter with no mode switch, no enforce branch, no issue-count increment, no `resolve_check_mode` call, and exit 0 on findings) over `core/deploy/tools/check-adr-flip.py` (**D-B: both**). **Wave D — DevTest rework** (see Mechanism). **Data change:** commit `8fc664d` flipped **23 ADR `status:` fields Proposed → Accepted** — 23 files, 23 insertions, 23 deletions, every changed line a status line.
**Mechanism:** git merge-commit (PR #3662 → main at `f0d95a58baeb67f7bbabf565259e6c128b79b153`). **Version-less by recorded determination** — D-Version condition **(B)**, milestone title is slug-only: **no tag claimed at Stage 12**, no version slot contended for, the Engineering-Commit-0 version re-verify and the Stage-12 atomic version claim both inapplicable, and **no GitHub Release published** (Surface 1 requires a tag). Follows the `public-flip-install-blockers` / `intake-elicitation-skill` / `domain-aware-stage5-design` / `memory-to-corpus-codification` / `cross-reference-integrity-ci` / `declarative-gating-model` version-less precedents. Phase B4 deploy = **no-op** (zero skill sources touched; no `.skill` rebuild). **Wave D fixed two real defects in work this release introduced, before merge.** Stage-7 **F1 (HIGH)**: Wave B single-sourced the scan *scope* across both callers but left the *allowlist* split — `link-check.yml` passed the tracked list carrying the CHANGELOG skip class while `deploy.sh` Check 14 passed only the operator-instance file, so two callers scanning byte-identical files returned different verdicts and the deploy-time baseline went **0 → 84** while CI stayed at 0; fixed by making `--allowlist` repeatable and UNION in argument order, with Check 14 passing the tracked base first and the instance file on top (measured after: deploy-time **84 → 0**, CI **0 → 0**; four coupled docs reconciled). Stage-7 **F2 (MEDIUM)**: Check 55's documented H2 exemption was **structurally unreachable in both accepted spellings** (`#242 …` swallowed by a blanket comment filter; `242 …` loaded under one key and looked up under another) and its self-test **passed vacuously** — it would still have passed with `load_exemptions` deleted; both axes were fixed (a tight `^#\d+\s+\S` comment carve-out; issue-ref key normalization on load and lookup) and the suite **de-vacuumed by mutation testing** — gutting the loader took the pre-fix suite from 15/15 PASS to a post-fix 18/21 with the three exemption cases correctly killed. Stage-7 **F3**: the fix was applied but **its causal claim did not survive verification** — the predicted `named-not-member` precision jump (12 → 2) did not occur; resolving all 11 refs individually found only **1** explained by closure, **7** belonging to no milestone and **3** to a different milestone, all genuine, so the all-states basis buys ~1 finding, not ~10. Cost was engineered down rather than accepted: fetching membership *through* `milestones(states:OPEN)` instead of scanning all 3232 repo issues is a **12.6× speedup** (16.5s → 1.3s), verified equivalent over the open-milestone population rather than assumed. Self-tests at the final head: work-hierarchy **21/21**, milestone-epic **19/19**, extraction-contract **9/9**, adr-flip **12/12**, adr-durability **30/30**, doc-links **11 fixtures**, release-links PASS. Every slice aligns a describing document to a capability the platform had already shipped. **#2472** — `release/references/pipeline/stage-04-planning.md`: the `domain:` class e.g.-list now names the shipped `process` guide, and the clause states which four domains ship a guide today (`software`, `governance`, `process`, `support`). **#2473** — `core/standards/sior-escalation-protocol.md` § Decision-Owner Mapping upgraded from free-text heuristic to **structured lookup**: select the Stakeholder Register row where `Decision Owner = yes` AND `Authority = <decision domain>` (Tracker 8 schema in `core/schemas/tracker-schemas.md` + the committed `operations/templates/stakeholder-register-template.csv`, shipped via #206), with the free-text `## Key People` path retained as the graceful-degradation fallback and warn-and-route-to-PgM as the terminal case; the residual note is rewritten — it no longer asserts the structured authority field is unshipped. **#2854** — three surfaces reconciled to the **two-tier CORRECTIONS model** (program `projects/_config/CORRECTIONS.md` = platform-wide redirects, read after CLAUDE.md; per-project `[Project]/CORRECTIONS.md` = project-scoped redirects, read after PROJECT.md, overriding the program tier on the same claim): `core/specs/operational-artifact-inventory.md` §8 (new two-tier lifecycle note + the narrower-is-safer scope test), `core/governance/OPERATIONS.md` § CORRECTIONS Graduation & Expiry Rule 1 (governs both tiers identically — tier placement is a scope test, not a lifecycle difference), and `core/disciplines/memory-architecture.md` (Learning-type row + §4 session-start read order). CLAUDE.md deliberately untouched — it already described the model (#241 shipped it). **#3289** — `core/standards/template-protocol.md` §6 P5(c) split into **(c-i)** `domain: project` with no plugin counterpart and **(c-ii)** `domain: software` whose required `canon:` names an external canon with no plugin counterpart, so a software no-plugin family (e.g. RFC / IETF 7322) finally has a satisfiable `canon_compat: none` path and no §6 family is stranded; `core/standards/template-taxonomy.md` §7 re-expressed as **convention-anchors** carrying the 2026-07-03 re-survey (`~/.claude/plugins/installed_plugins.json` + the official-marketplace roster found the `engineering:*` / `product-management:*` / `operations:*` suites **absent**), reconciling the L173/L190 "installed" assertions with the Row-9 addendum. **#2805** — `operations/skills/comms-writer/references/voice-guide.md` gains an Executive Compression & Concreteness section + 2 anti-pattern rows; `operations/skills/comms-writer/SKILL.md` gains a 5-step compression pass on the Executive-brief type (cause→clause; consequence→concrete+clock; impact→executive unit; expected-vs-unexpected; cut a paragraph) and 2 five-field Domain-Specific Failure Modes (abstract-consequence-where-a-concrete-one-is-knowable → **OUT**; partially-expected-outcome-framed-as-pure-defect → **INPUT**). **#3385** — `operations/skills/artifact-lint/SKILL.md` Check 4 keys the displaced-content rule on the **ADR-080 folder union** (`promotion_state: promoted ⇒ folder ∉ {08-generated, _generated}`), so a promoted-but-not-moved artifact is caught in EITHER staging bin across the taxonomy-migration window; the issue's "ADR-079" citation was a misattribution and is corrected to ADR-080 in both the edit and the plan. **#3386** — `operations/skills/daily-status/SKILL.md` + `operations/skills/weekly-status-rollup/SKILL.md` carry an explicit **read-side aggregation contract**: aggregate from the tracked (Domain-B) layer — `DEC-###` / `ACT-###` / `MTG-###` entries, `MSG-##` rows, RAID rows — and cite each entry's `source_inputs` / `source_ref` provenance rather than re-scanning raw transcripts; daily-status' single same-day triggering-transcript intake is preserved as the deliberate **write-side** exception.
**Mechanism:** git merge-commit (PR #3667 → main at `a0fe5e61de1894239133c18e55f9d1da63819f86`; **`--admin` override used** — base-branch policy prohibits a plain merge even when the PR is CLEAN/MERGEABLE, CI green 34/34 at merge). The atomic ref-CAS then computed next-free = **`v3.82`** against the mainline anchor and pushed a **signed-annotated** tag at the merge SHA — **won on first attempt, no collision**. **Phase B4 deploy is NOT a no-op** (unlike v3.79–v3.81): this release touches skill sources, and **10 `.skill` packages were rebuilt** — **4 for the direct skill edits** (`comms-writer` + `artifact-lint` for #2805/#3385; `daily-status` + `weekly-status-rollup` for #3386) and **6 consumer packages** (`delivery-engine`, `eval-writer`, `pmo-process-designer`, `pmo-skill-refiner`, `project-initiator`, `release-planner`) that bundle the two canonicals #3289 edited (`template-taxonomy.md`, `template-protocol.md`) via build-time injection. #### Deployment Log v3.73.1
**Files deployed:** Delivery-path hotfix for the v3.73 security patch — `update.sh` did not refresh the deployed `.claude/hooks` bundle, so a hook/helper security fix (e.g. the v3.73 GHSA-g9g6 awk-primitive co-deploy) never reached an already-installed workspace via `update.sh`; only a full `setup-workspace.sh` re-run delivered it (#3430). Fixed as a single branch/PR/merge (PR #3431, `fix/3430-update-refresh-hooks → main`, GitHub-signed squash commit at `421658d395a207d6525f596cbf71440d115127ac`, 2026-07-12T21:59Z). Release Class: **hotfix** (code + one regression test + CI wiring only — no skill/governance/schema/ADR change). Adds `setup-workspace.sh --refresh-hooks`: a non-interactive, checksum-aware refresh of ONLY the security-hook bundle (hooks + co-shipped primitives + `lib/`), reusing the single-sourced `install_hooks` co-deploy list — an unedited platform hook (matches its recorded baseline, or has none) is refreshed to source; an operator-edited hook (diverged from baseline) is preserved + warned, never clobbered; `INSTALL_COMPLETE=1` prevents the EXIT-trap from rolling back the co-deployed primitives. Adds an `update.sh` Phase 5c that delegates to it and flips the "changed" flag ONLY on a genuinely REFRESHED hook, preserving the `EX_NOCHANGE` (64) no-op contract (the primitives' plain-`cp` logs `INSTALLED` even when byte-identical). No `.skill` package rebuild (no skill files touched). Regression: new 7-case `core/deploy/tests/test_refresh_hooks.sh` (stale-hook refresh · never-deployed-primitive co-deploy · operator `.mode` preserved · operator-edited-hook preserved+warned · no-op zero-`REFRESHED` · `--dry-run` no-mutation · missing-workspace fail-closed), wired into `install-tests.yml`; GHSA-g9g6 hook suite 23/23 and install end-to-end 12/0 both unchanged.
**Mechanism:** git squash-merge (PR #3431 → main, `--admin --squash`; branch protection required admin override past the signed-commits + conversation-resolution policy, CI green — 27/27 check-runs success) + the `v3.73.1` tag + published GitHub Release placed at the Stage-13 close-out commit (which carries the `.version` bump to `v3.73.1` alongside the corpus). Patch path from `v3.73`; dry-run confirmed next-free = `v3.73.1`, no collision.
**Timestamp:** 2026-07-12 CDT (fix merge 2026-07-12T21:59:01Z UTC; tag at Stage-13 close-out SHA)
**Outcome:** SUCCESS

#### Deployment Log v3.73
**Files deployed:** Coordinated-disclosure security patch for two advisories — **GHSA-g9g6-28c9-vrx5** (LOW, CWE-693) and **GHSA-fxcr-7x63-3g94** (MEDIUM, CWE-59/200/352) — bundled into one release PR #3426 (`release/security-g9g6-fxcr → main`, squash-merged at `874f59abee96965a4561a032bb186d7d2377715f`, 2026-07-12T21:00Z). Release Class: **security-patch**. **GHSA-g9g6:** the warn/enforce hooks `block-gh-path-leak.sh` + `block-fragile-refs.sh` fail-opened (`exit 0`) when a co-shipped internal primitive (`path-leak-patterns.sh` / `lib/positional-issueref.awk`) was absent — regardless of `.mode`, including enforce — and `positional-issueref.awk` was never co-deployed (its detector had never run on a deployed workspace). Fixed by extending the GHSA-9cjm / ADR-078 mode-coupled posture to internal primitives (enforce fail-closed `exit 2`, warn/off degrade), verifying the primitive API (`command -v` / an awk canary, not mere file existence), a caller-owned `exit 2`, and co-deploying the awk in `setup-workspace.sh` + `setup-ci-layout.sh`; ADR-078 amended (D6–D8 + accepted over-block residual). **GHSA-fxcr:** the eval-viewer generator (`release/skills/pmo-skill-refiner/eval-viewer/generate_review.py`) followed symlinks + hardlinks out of the workspace (CWE-59/22), embedded secrets verbatim (CWE-200/532), and accepted unauthenticated writes (CWE-352). Fixed by confining every read to the resolved workspace root + rejecting hardlinks, size-capping + best-effort-redacting embedded text (incl. `.svg`/`.env`/no-extension text files), and gating `POST /api/feedback` behind a loopback `Host` + same-origin `Origin` + a per-run CSRF token (`hmac.compare_digest`). `pmo-skill-refiner.skill` rebuilt at close-out. Verified via three adversarial refutation passes + a clean confirmation sweep; new regression tests (hook 23 cases + eval-viewer 47 cases) + a focused `eval-viewer-security` CI job; full hook suite 426/0. The build-security-hardening milestone (durable CI prevention gates for these classes) is handled separately.
**Mechanism:** git squash-merge (PR #3426 → main, GitHub-signed squash commit at `874f59ab`; CI green — 27/27 check-runs success; `--admin` past the signed-commits + conversation-resolution branch policy). The `v3.73` tag + published GitHub Release are placed at the Stage-13 close-out commit (which carries the rebuilt `pmo-skill-refiner.skill` alongside the source fix, so the tagged package is not the vulnerable eval-viewer). One CodeQL alert dismissed as a test-fixture false positive (`used in tests` — fake tokens exercising the redaction pass).
**Timestamp:** 2026-07-12 CDT (merge 2026-07-12T21:00:52Z UTC; tag at merge SHA)
**Outcome:** SUCCESS

#### Release Learnings v2.22
**Surprise:** the milestone entered Stage 4 recorded version-less, but at Stage 5 Collective Review the version-less posture was reconsidered and the release settled on **v2.22 (minor)** — all three cards make material edits to 5 skills whose `version:` field MUST bump to a release tag per `version-field-semantics.md` (a milestone slug is regex-invalid against `^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(-[a-z]+)?$`), so there was no valid version-less provision once the skill-bump surface was in scope.
**Would-change:** surface the skill-`version:`-bump implication at the Stage 4 D-Version gate, not at Stage 5 — the deciding fact (5 skills bump, therefore a tag is mandatory) was knowable from the File Change Matrix at planning time; pulling it forward would have settled the version one stage earlier.
**Watch-for:** the **cite-by-path** single-sourcing choice (comms-writer Types 2/3 + `channel-formats.md` + the `MTG-###` field all point at the two new canonical specs rather than carrying inline copies) voids the duplicate-source-discipline drift risk by construction — but it means any future edit to the agenda/recap element set must land in the canonical spec only; an editor who "fixes" the format inline in a consuming skill re-introduces the very duplication this release removed. Also watch the **#56 decompose**: the foundation ships one domain (Estimation) seeded with its consuming skill — the 9 deferred #1173 domains must each preserve that seed-with-consumer floor to avoid an orphaned-corpus maintenance-debt class.

**Files deployed:** No runtime-skill or harness file is deployed by this release's own mechanism — the changed set is **release-tooling + governance/reference-corpus + one regression test + one CI-workflow wiring + the `.version` source file**, with **no `SKILL.md` and no `packages/` touched** (deploy no-op; the only mutated runtime-adjacent surfaces are `core/deploy/deploy.sh` and `release/tools/automated-closeout.sh`, which are scripts the repo ships, not deployed skill mirrors). The 3-layer fix: **(L1)** corrects the stale value (now tracks the shipped release line on `main`); **(L2)** makes the `.version` stamp an owned Stage 13 close-out step — a new `phase_bump_version` in `release/tools/automated-closeout.sh` (with dry-run/PR-body wiring and `.version` added to the chore-commit staging list) plus declaring `.version` in the `release/references/pipeline/stage-13-close.md` output set + Phase B mechanism; **(L3)** adds `core/deploy/deploy.sh` **Check 39** asserting `.version` matches the latest reachable release tag (FAILs ≥2 minors behind, tolerates the legitimate 1-version Stage 12→13 window as WARN), with regression coverage in `core/deploy/tests/test_version_stamping.sh` wired into the suite via `.github/workflows/install-tests.yml`, and a `docs/UPDATE.md` clarification that `.version` is release-cut-owned. **Version-less identity:** milestone `release-version-stamping` (Release Class A — Routine) ships version-less per operator decision (Stage 4, 2026-06-20) following the `public-flip-install-blockers` / `intake-elicitation-skill` / `domain-aware-stage5-design` / `memory-to-corpus-codification` / `cross-reference-integrity-ci` precedents — no `vX.Y` assigned to this release, no signed-annotated tag cut, and no GitHub Release. Corpus close-out (RELEASE_LOG `DEPLOYED → VERIFIED` + INDEX + DIGEST + RELEASE_NOTES) via this Stage 13 chore PR — hand-authored because `automated-closeout.sh` is version-keyed and cannot run for a version-less release (the documented Phase-B chore-PR mechanism).

No tag (version-less).

provenance = the *upstream human evidence* an artifact derives from, with `source_inputs` scoped out as a separate provenance concept not yet in-schema), records the **field-vs-verb composition note** (the scalars compose against the 7 MVP `relationships[]` verbs rather than extending them — no new enum value, the 7-MVP-type constraint unchanged), and specifies **sidecar `.meta.yml` parity** (the lineage fields carry identically on non-markdown artifacts so edges stay grep-discoverable) plus a **WARN-not-BLOCK dangling-lineage rule** (a lineage path that does not resolve is a flag, consistent with relationship-target resolution); and reconciles `core/standards/template-protocol.md` §9 (`topic` → `sibling_topic` — the third reconcile file caught at Stage 5, see FINDING below). **#1165 (artifact-lint — NEW skill):** authors `operations/skills/artifact-lint/SKILL.md`, a NEW Autonomy-Tier-1 **recommend-only** skill that scans a project's generated-artifact surface (`08-Generated/` + promoted folders) and runs **five graph-integrity checks — orphan / sibling-duplicate / stale-draft / displaced-content / version-chain** — reading the #334 lineage frontmatter, staging a single report to `08-Generated/artifact-lint-YYYY-MM-DD.md` with per-finding proposed action + reversibility tier + confidence + cited evidence, and performing **no file moves and no deletes** (the operator approves every action); ships `operations/skills/artifact-lint/references/work-plan-taxonomy.md` (the **17-row taxonomy** — Canonical-10 ≡ the Domain-C synthesis `artifact_type` set + Situational-7 ≡ the plan-type set + waterfall/cutover rows — that the displaced-content Check 4 uses as the canonical-target-folder lookup) and `references/edge-cases.md`; #### Deployment Log v1.24
**Files deployed:** No runtime-skill *or* harness file is deployed by this release's own mechanism — the changed set is **two K1/reference docs (`release/references/pipeline/stage-02-triage.md`, `release/governance/release-process.md`) + one CI-gate allowlist (`core/hooks/reference-durability-allowlist.txt`)**, with **no `SKILL.md`, no `packages/`, and no `core/rules/` mirror or hook logic touched** (deploy no-op — no `./core/deploy/deploy.sh --deploy` skill-mirror sync required; mirrors the v1.22 text/tool-only profile). The release (single PR #1068, single-issue `routine` release, single-branch D-C SINGLE) resolves the three v1.02-triage carry-forward findings on issue #309. **CF-2 (awk silent-zero):** the Stage-2 Triage A6.5 management-task detection table in `stage-02-triage.md` used `awk '/^### Dependencies/,/^### /'` (and the parallel `/^### Priority/,/^### /`) — a non-functional range, because the start regex is itself matched by the end regex `^### `, collapsing the range to the heading line so the body is dropped and the downstream `grep` returns nothing. All **3 affected instances** — Pattern 1b orphaned-dependency extraction, Pattern 2a P1-blocked-by-lower dependency extraction, Pattern 2a blocker-priority extraction — were replaced with the robust `awk 'BEGIN{flag=0} /^### <heading>/{flag=1;next} /^### /{flag=0} flag'` form (table-cell pipe-escaping preserved; Pattern 4a — a `grep -cE`, no awk — correctly untouched, which also corrected the issue body's stale "1b/2a/4a" instance inventory). **CF-3 (citation drift):** the Stage-2 Triage-cadence paragraph in `release-process.md` cited the sister management-task spec's "Stale issues" detection at `Phase A4 (ii) detection-pattern (1a)` — a stale anchor (the pattern lives at `Phase A6.5 Pattern (1a)`; Phase A4 there is the unrelated feasibility quick-check) — corrected to `Phase A6.5 Pattern (1a)` (the underlying P1-SLA bilateral contract is intact; only the anchor was wrong). **CF-1 (yq toolchain) DE-SCOPED:** the `yq` dependency targeted the **G3-13 Roadmap-Cascade Validation** detection bash in `core/schemas/gate-criteria-spec.md`, which ADR-012 (roadmap-instance-descope, Accepted 2026-06-02) already retired — no live detection bash remains carrying the dependency, so CF-1 was evidence-closed at verification, not engineered. **Three CI-gate remediations rode along** on the touched lines: the operator-handle reference on the re-touched A6.5 lines was tokenized, an `allow-link` marker was added (per-file reference-durability), and a `stage-02-triage.md` entry was added to `core/hooks/reference-durability-allowlist.txt` so the issue-ref detector does not false-positive on two pre-existing `#`-shaped patterns the awk-fix + tokenization re-surfaced in the added-lines delta (the `#5-process` anchor fragment of a `[Phase B B3](#5-process)` link, and the `#[0-9]+` grep regex inside the detection bash — an anchor fragment and a regex, NOT net-new fragile refs; mirrors the sibling stage-03 / stage-04 / stage-12 allowlist entries). Corpus close-out (RELEASE_NOTES + LOG `DEPLOYED → VERIFIED` + INDEX + DIGEST + CHANGELOG) lands at the Stage 13 chore PR.
**Mechanism:** git merge (PR #1068 → main, `--merge`) + signed-annotated tag push (Tag-SHA-Direct pattern per stage-12-execute.md § Phase B; the `v1.24` tag points at the merge SHA `10977af642da65c1bbb2b35332ff3cba70cf5bb4`; ED25519 signature valid — `git tag -v v1.24` reports a Good signature; zero signing bypasses). No skill-package rebuild and no `./core/deploy/deploy.sh --deploy` — the changed set carries no `skills/`, `packages/`, or harness paths, so there is nothing to deploy or re-sync (deploy no-op). **Re-versioned planned v1.21 → v1.24:** authored/branched/PR-titled as `v1.21` (`release/v1.21-pipeline-detection-bash-debt`); `v1.21`/`v1.22`/`v1.23` were each claimed by concurrent releases (governance-as-code-quality-gates = v1.21, deploy-toolchain-defect-cleanup = v1.22, pmo-skill-reference-substrate = v1.23) between this release's planning and execute, so it landed at next-free **v1.24** (the #769 version-collision class). #### Deployment Log v1.17
**Files deployed:** `comms-writer` + `artifact-generator` skills (SKILL.md + references/) and their two rebuilt `.skill` packages (`packages/comms-writer.skill`, `packages/artifact-generator.skill`) — deployed post-merge of this Stage 13 chore PR via `./core/deploy/deploy.sh --deploy comms-writer artifact-generator`. Single release PR #864 merge (5-issue `novel` release, D-C SINGLE; sequenced #174 → #173 → #176 → #175 → #791). The release narrows the two PMO authoring skills to their PMO-unique surface, gives them an Anthropic-voice offload path that runs at design time (no runtime Anthropic dependency), and resolves a duplicate-ADR collision. **#174 (narrow comms-writer to 6 PMO-unique primary types):** `comms-writer` SKILL.md narrowed to the 6 communication types that are genuinely PMO-unique, with the PMO-critical rules (audience calibration, escalation discipline, status-theater guardrails) preserved rather than dropped in the narrowing. **#173 (comms-writer owns exec-brief + stakeholder-email — own-with-harvest):** `comms-writer` takes ownership of executive-brief and stakeholder-email generation under the **own-with-harvest** pattern — the Anthropic voice is harvested into the skill at design time, so generation carries no runtime Anthropic dependency. **#176 (narrow artifact-generator catalog — Part A+B):** the `artifact-generator` catalog narrowed to its PMO-unique artifact set (the catalog now enumerates 27 artifacts after the narrowing + the own-with-harvest additions), removing the commodity artifacts that an Anthropic scaffolding skill produces better. **#175 (artifact-generator Anthropic offload Wrapper Mode + Part-D routing tree — Part C+D):** a NEW **Wrapper Mode** in `artifact-generator` that offloads commodity artifact scaffolding to an Anthropic skill, plus a **Part-D routing decision tree** in `CLAUDE.md` that routes an artifact request to the PMO catalog vs. the Anthropic offload path. **#791 (resolve duplicate ADR-022 collision):** the duplicate `ADR-022` collision in `core/ADRs/` is resolved by renumbering the skill-sourcing-coupling-posture ADR to **`ADR-023-skill-sourcing-coupling-posture.md`** (the platform-config-vs-operator-toml-split ADR retains `ADR-022`), with the cross-references reconciled and the skill-sourcing registry trigger-(d) wired. Engineering Commit 0 landed the release plan `release/releases/plans/v1/v1.17_RELEASE_PLAN.md`. Corpus close-out (RELEASE_NOTES + LOG `DEPLOYED → VERIFIED` + INDEX + DIGEST + CHANGELOG) via this Stage 13 chore PR.

**Result:** SUCCESS — release PR #864 merged to main via merge commit `79d8827d0fb57f671ad49c0a80acf9e376bc5a55` (D-C SINGLE; `git revert -m 1` rollback convention); signed-annotated `v1.17` tag points at the merge SHA; zero signing bypasses; the two rebuilt `.skill` packages (`comms-writer.skill`, `artifact-generator.skill`) are staged from scrubbed source so package↔source parity (Check 7) holds on main. **Outcome:** ALIGNED — finalized at Stage 13 VERIFIED (set by the Stage 13 chore PR per decision-outcome-tracking.md). Post-deploy main exhibits the AFTER state: `comms-writer` covers the 6 PMO-unique communication types and owns exec-brief + stakeholder-email generation via the own-with-harvest pattern (Anthropic voice harvested at design time, no runtime Anthropic dependency); `artifact-generator` enumerates a 27-artifact PMO-unique catalog and offloads commodity scaffolding through its new Wrapper Mode, with a Part-D routing decision tree in `CLAUDE.md` directing an artifact request to the PMO catalog vs. the Anthropic offload path; and the duplicate-`ADR-022` collision is resolved (`ADR-023-skill-sourcing-coupling-posture.md` + reconciled cross-references + registry trigger-(d)). `novel` release class, CHEAP reversibility (single `git revert -m 1` of the release PR — skill-scope narrowing + offload wrapper + routing tree + ADR renumber; no data migration; re-run `./deploy.sh --deploy` post-revert to re-sync skill mirrors), 30-day Stage 13 outcome window.
**Learnings:** (1) **In-pipeline re-version under concurrent releases** — this release was authored as `v1.15` but two concurrent releases (`platform-self-measurement-and-quality-method` as v1.15, `delivery-capacity-and-lifecycle-gating` as v1.16) claimed the lower numbers first, forcing a re-version to `v1.17`; the reusable lesson (already filed as #769 from v1.13) is that the Stage 12 / Phase A6.5 pre-merge check must re-verify the version number is still unclaimed, not just file/gate divergence. (2) **In-release ADR-collision resolution** — a duplicate `ADR-022` basename existed across two ADRs; resolving it in-release by renumbering the later-authored ADR to `ADR-023` (with cross-reference + registry reconciliation) is cleaner than shipping the collision and filing a fast-follow, because an ADR number is a durable reference other docs cite.

#### Deployment Log v1.16
**Files deployed:** Four NEW reference docs under `operations/skills/delivery-engine/references/` (`capacity-model.md`, `estimation-standards.md`, `lifecycle-stages.md`, `gate-definitions.md`) + the `delivery-engine` SKILL.md mode wiring (Modes C/D/E/F/G consume the new docs) + a surgical single-source edit to `operations/skills/delivery-engine/references/sprint-defaults.md` + the rebuilt `packages/delivery-engine.skill` package, all via release PR #859 merge (4-issue `novel` release; D-C SINGLE; sequenced #272 → #273 → #274 → #275). The release turns the delivery-engine's sprint-planning + release-readiness surfaces from rules-of-thumb into governed method across capacity, estimation, lifecycle, and gates. **#272 (capacity-model.md):** a NEW reference modeling *effective* delivery-team capacity — the capacity formula (§1), context-switching penalties across concurrent projects (§3), Brooks's-Law late-addition thresholds (§4), the 60/20/20 effort-allocation split (§5), team-stability + vendor-ramp thresholds (§6–§7), staffing-concentration / bus-factor risk (§8), and the demand-versus-supply gap RAG bands (§9); wired into `delivery-engine` Mode D (capacity formula + penalties) + Mode E (demand-supply gap). Two inputs are referenced not restated — the focus factor (owned by `estimation-standards.md` §3) and the platform bus-factor rule (owned by `core/disciplines/km-protocols.md` §4). **#273 (estimation-standards.md):** a NEW reference, the canonical home for estimation discipline — Cone-of-Uncertainty band widths bounding precision by lifecycle phase (§1, normative cone rule rejects an over-tight range), planning-horizon commit-vs-forecast rules, the **canonical focus-factor table this doc now solely owns** (§3, promoted unchanged from its sole prior occurrence in `sprint-defaults.md` §1.2 — the single-source move), the three-zone buffer model, the velocity-and-estimates-as-ranges enforcement rule (§5), and the contingency-vs-management-reserve distinction; wired into Mode D (bands + horizon + focus-factor table + buffer model) + Mode E (velocity-as-range enforcement). **#274 (lifecycle-stages.md):** a NEW reference enumerating the **15-stage universal delivery lifecycle** (the temporal axis), five-phase super-grouped (Capture → Prepare → Build → Validate → Deliver), with per-stage entry/exit criteria + consumed/produced artifacts and a 5-of-8 methodology mapping; wired into Mode C (execution-stage entry criteria) + Mode F (validation-stage exit criteria, incl. the QA/Acceptance exit predicate that blocks Plan Review while a P1 defect is open). Carries an explicit boundary statement (project delivery lifecycle ≠ the platform's own release pipeline). **#275 (gate-definitions.md):** a NEW reference, the canonical sequence of **eleven project-lifecycle gates** — `LG-0` (Idea Screen) through `LG-10` (Closure) — each with entry/exit criteria, key decisions, authority holder, required artifacts, and escalation path; wired into Mode F (per-gate exit criteria + transition rule) + Mode G (gate-decision attribution to gate + authority holder) + Mode C (situates sprint DoR as LG-4). Cross-references the sibling gate-TYPE taxonomy (`gate-checklists.md`) rather than restating it; the `LG-` prefix is deliberately namespaced to avoid collision with the release-pipeline's `G-PR*/G-EX*/G-CL*` stage gates. The three governance docs (`lifecycle-stages.md` temporal axis · `gate-definitions.md` gate-instance axis · `gate-checklists.md` gate-type axis) form a clean three-axis model, each owning one axis and cross-referencing the others. Engineering Commit 0 landed the release plan `release/releases/plans/v1/v1.16_RELEASE_PLAN.md`. Corpus close-out (RELEASE_NOTES + LOG `DEPLOYED → VERIFIED` + INDEX + DIGEST) via the combined Stage 12+13 chore PR.
**Mechanism:** git merge (PR #859 → main) + signed-annotated tag push (Tag-SHA-Direct pattern per stage-12-execute.md § Phase B; the `v1.16` tag points at the merge SHA `a915daa94caa68423b42cbf7e7db69a4dfbc37e1`; zero signing bypasses). **Skill-package rebuild only — runtime skill-copy deferred per content-release precedent:** the changed `delivery-engine` SKILL.md + its 4 new + 1 edited `references/` docs have the `.skill` package rebuilt and staged in `packages/` so Check 7 (package↔source parity) passes on main; the canonical deployment for this content release is the merge + signed tag, and the runtime skill-copy `./deploy.sh --deploy` is deferred (the operator runs the runtime mirror sync when convenient — the reference docs activate on the merge, git-native, per the v1.11 / v1.13 / v1.15 precedent).
**Timestamp:** 2026-06-13 (PR #859 merge; MERGE_SHA `a915daa94caa68423b42cbf7e7db69a4dfbc37e1`)
**Cycle-Time:** N/A — no gate-outcome/plan-review-go event for v1.16 in the pipeline event log; no deployment-status/deploy-skill or deploy-harness event recorded (repo carries skill source + rebuilt package; runtime skill-copy deferred; mechanism: compute-cycle-time.sh)
**Result:** SUCCESS — release PR #859 merged to main via merge commit `a915daa94caa68423b42cbf7e7db69a4dfbc37e1` (D-C SINGLE; `git revert -m 1` rollback convention); signed-annotated `v1.16` tag points at the merge SHA; zero signing bypasses; the rebuilt `delivery-engine.skill` package is staged from scrubbed source so package↔source parity (Check 7) holds on main. A concurrent v1.15 merged into main mid-pipeline; the resulting conflict on the `delivery-engine` SKILL.md was resolved at Stage 12 with both edit-sets (v1.15 RCA additions + this release's Mode C/D/E/F/G wiring) preserved. Two CI gates (durable-corpus `#N` fragility) flagged at Stage 9 Plan Review were de-fragiled before merge.
**Outcome:** ALIGNED — finalized at Stage 13 VERIFIED (combined Stage 12+13 close; CI green, 4 docs confirmed on main at the merge SHA, signed `v1.16` tag valid). Post-deploy main exhibits the AFTER state: `delivery-engine` Mode D sizes sprint scope against an effective-capacity figure (context-switch / Brooks / effort-split discounted) and against the Cone-of-Uncertainty bands + focus-factor table; Mode E enforces velocity-and-estimates-as-ranges and reads the demand-supply gap; Mode C/F resolve a work item to a named lifecycle stage + lifecycle gate with entry/exit criteria; and Mode G attributes gate decisions to the correct `LG-` gate and authority holder. `novel` release class, CHEAP reversibility (single `git revert -m 1` of the release PR — additive reference docs + mode wiring + one-line sprint-defaults single-source edit; no data migration), 30-day Stage 13 outcome window.
**Learnings:** (1) **Concurrent-release SKILL.md conflict** — a concurrent v1.15 merging into main mid-pipeline produced a same-file conflict on `delivery-engine/SKILL.md` (both releases edited it); the reusable lesson is that two in-flight releases touching the same skill SKILL.md must resolve at the later release's Stage 12 with BOTH edit-sets preserved (keep-both, not last-writer-wins), then rebuild the `.skill` package against the merged source so package↔source parity holds. (2) **Durable-corpus fragility caught at Stage 9** — two CI gates flagged a durable-corpus fragile-reference (`#N`) at Plan Review; the reusable lesson is that the durable-corpus fragile-reference gate fires on new reference docs that carry bare issue refs in prose, and de-fragiling (allow-link marker or by-name rewrite) belongs at Stage 9 before merge, not as a post-merge fast-follow.

#### Release Learnings v1.01

**Surprise:** R-A scope expansion adopted at Stage 5 Collective Review (initial scope was the Stage 12 spec only; expanded to scrub all 7 file locations carrying the prior lightweight tag form) — re-classified the release from `routine` to `cross-cutting`. Cheaper-to-stricter transition was correctly applied; per-action signatures already rendered remained valid.

**Would-change:** The `core/CLAUDE.md.template` tier-selection wording sync was logged as Tier 1 out-of-scope drift at Stage 5 (kept under cross-cutting (b) threshold) but emerged as a follow-up Issue at Stage 13. A more aggressive R-A scope expansion at Stage 5 (or a Stage 4 R-A scope audit gate) might have caught the template surface earlier. Net: deferral was correct under the threshold discipline; mechanism for future R-A scope audits could surface template/generator surfaces explicitly.

**Watch-for:** First downstream release after v1.01-intake will be the first consumer of (a) the G-PR8 mid-pipeline divergence check at Stage 9 Phase A6.5, (b) the AC-Drift Handling Protocol verdict enum at Stage 8, and (c) the new `Outcome:` line on the Deployment Log per #218 tracking. Both #105 and #274 carry explicit cutover clauses exempting v1.01-intake itself. If the first downstream release has a clean Stage 9 entry (no concurrent release mid-pipeline) and clean Stage 8 (no drifted ACs), G-PR8 + Drift-protocol firing remains unobserved — empirical validation deferred until a representative test case emerges in production. The reflexive-pipeline-loop discipline is now an established pattern across 3 cutover-clause subjects in a single release; reusable convention for future protocol-shipping releases.
